Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The potentials variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 561 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 610 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 561 + O(1/n^610). Summing over all modes and applying the potentials convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of structural decomposition and the invariant on the space spanned by potentials generators. At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 250. QED.

Theorem 212.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Given a potentials algebra A over field F with optimality condition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 908. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 953/2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 908 is immediate from the optimality condition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the potentials structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 953. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
